908	class MiniCPMLightningMixer(nn.Module):
909	    """Lightning attention mixer that uses SimpleGLAAttnBackend.
910	
911	    This is a wrapper that prepares inputs for the backend and handles
912	    the QKV projection, normalization, RoPE, and output processing,
913	    while delegating the Simple GLA kernel calls to SimpleGLAAttnBackend.
914	    """
915	
916	    def __init__(
917	        self,
918	        hidden_size: int,
919	        num_heads: int,
920	        num_kv_heads: int,
921	        head_dim: int,
922	        layer_id: int = 0,
923	        rope_theta: float = 10000,
924	        rope_scaling: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
925	        max_position_embeddings: int = 8192,
926	        quant_config: Optional[QuantizationConfig] = None,
927	        prefix: str = "",
928	        use_rope: bool = True,
929	        use_output_gate: bool = False,
930	        attention_bias: bool = False,
931	        rms_norm_eps: float = 1e-6,
932	        use_output_norm: bool = False,
933	        qk_norm: bool = True,
934	        rope_head_dim: Optional[int] = None,
935	        scale: str = "1/sqrt(d)",
936	    ) -> None:
937	        super().__init__()
938	        self.hidden_size = hidden_size
939	        tp_size = get_tensor_model_parallel_world_size()
940	        self.total_num_heads = num_heads
941	        assert self.total_num_heads % tp_size == 0
942	        self.num_heads = self.total_num_heads // tp_size
943	        self.total_num_kv_heads = num_kv_heads
944	        if self.total_num_kv_heads >= tp_size:
945	            assert self.total_num_kv_heads % tp_size == 0
946	        else:
947	            assert tp_size % self.total_num_kv_heads == 0
948	        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, self.total_num_kv_heads // tp_size)
949	        self.head_dim = head_dim
950	        if scale == "1/sqrt(d)":
951	            self.scale = self.head_dim ** (-0.5)
952	        elif scale == "1/d":
953	            self.scale = self.head_dim ** (-1.0)
954	        else:
955	            self.scale = 1.0
956	        self.use_output_gate = use_output_gate
957	        self.attention_bias = attention_bias
958	        self.rms_norm_eps = rms_norm_eps
959	        self.use_rope = use_rope
960	        self.qk_norm = qk_norm
961	        self.use_output_norm = use_output_norm
962	        self.rope_head_dim = (
963	            rope_head_dim if rope_head_dim is not None else self.head_dim
964	        )
965	        assert self.rope_head_dim <= self.head_dim
966	
967	        self.q_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
968	        self.kv_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
969	        self.rope_theta = rope_theta
970	        self.max_position_embeddings = max_position_embeddings
971	
972	        self.qkv_proj = QKVParallelLinear(
973	            hidden_size,
974	            self.head_dim,
975	            self.total_num_heads,
976	            self.total_num_kv_heads,
977	            bias=False,
978	            quant_config=quant_config,
979	            prefix=add_prefix("qkv_proj", prefix),
980	        )
981	
982	        self.o_proj = RowParallelLinear(
983	            self.total_num_heads * self.head_dim,
984	            hidden_size,
985	            bias=False,
986	            quant_config=quant_config,
987	            prefix=add_prefix("o_proj", prefix),
988	        )
989	
990	        if self.use_output_norm:
991	            self.o_norm = RMSNorm(self.num_heads * self.head_dim, eps=self.rms_norm_eps)
992	
993	        if self.use_output_gate:
994	            self.z_proj = ColumnParallelLinear(
995	                self.hidden_size,
996	                self.total_num_heads * self.head_dim,
997	                bias=self.attention_bias,
998	                quant_config=quant_config,
999	                prefix=add_prefix("z_proj", prefix),
1000	            )
1001	
1002	        if self.qk_norm:
1003	            self.q_norm = RMSNorm(self.head_dim, eps=self.rms_norm_eps)
1004	            self.k_norm = RMSNorm(self.head_dim, eps=self.rms_norm_eps)
1005	
1006	        if self.use_rope:

1019	        self,
1020	        positions: torch.Tensor,

1024	        s, e = _evt_record("gla_qkv_proj_ms"); qkv, _ = self.qkv_proj(hidden_states); _evt_end(e)
1025	
1026	        # GLA qk_norm + rope fast paths.
1027	        # OOP variant (preferred): triton kernel reads qkv, writes q/k/v as 3
1028	        # SEPARATE contig outputs in one launch — no split/contig copy.
1029	        # In-place variant: TRT-LLM CUDA kernel modifies qkv in place; needs
1030	        # split + .contiguous() ×3 (~8.74 ms / chunk).
1031	        # Eligibility: qk_norm + rope, NH_q == NH_kv, head_dim ∈ {64,128,256},
1032	        # no yarn (rope_scaling None), qkv bf16 cuda contig, neox style,
1033	        # rotary_dim == head_dim.
1034	        used_fused_qknorm_rope = False
1035	        _gla_can_fuse = (
1036	            self.qk_norm
1037	            and self.use_rope
1038	            and self.num_heads == self.num_kv_heads
1039	            and self.head_dim in (64, 128, 256)
1040	            and self.rope_head_dim == self.head_dim
1041	            and qkv.dtype == torch.bfloat16
1042	            and qkv.is_cuda
1043	            and qkv.is_contiguous()
1044	            and getattr(self.rotary_emb, "is_neox_style", True)
1045	            and getattr(self.rotary_emb, "rotary_dim", self.head_dim) == self.head_dim
1046	        )
1047	        if (
1048	            _MINICPM_FUSED_GLA_QK_NORM_ROPE_OOP
1049	            and _gla_can_fuse
1050	            and _minicpm_fused_qknorm_rope_oop is not None
1051	            and getattr(self.rotary_emb, "cos_sin_cache", None) is not None
1052	        ):
1053	            s, e = _evt_record("gla_fused_qk_norm_rope_ms")
1054	            cos_sin = self.rotary_emb.cos_sin_cache  # (max_pos, D) fp32
1055	            q, k, v = _minicpm_fused_qknorm_rope_oop(
1056	                qkv,
1057	                self.q_norm.weight,
1058	                self.k_norm.weight,
1059	                cos_sin,
1060	                positions,
1061	                self.num_heads,
1062	                self.num_kv_heads,
1063	                self.num_kv_heads,
1064	                self.head_dim,
1065	                float(self.rms_norm_eps),
1066	            )
1067	            # q/k/v already (T, NH, D) contig — bypass split/reshape downstream.
1068	            _evt_end(e)
1069	            used_fused_qknorm_rope = True
1070	            # mark with skip flag so we don't re-reshape
1071	            _gla_oop_done = True
1072	        elif (
1073	            _MINICPM_FUSED_GLA_QK_NORM_ROPE
1074	            and _fused_qk_norm_rope is not None
1075	            and _gla_can_fuse
1076	        ):
1077	            s, e = _evt_record("gla_fused_qk_norm_rope_ms")
1078	            pos_i32 = positions if positions.dtype == torch.int32 else positions.to(torch.int32)
1079	            _fused_qk_norm_rope(
1080	                qkv,
1081	                self.num_heads, self.num_kv_heads, self.num_kv_heads,
1082	                self.head_dim, float(self.rms_norm_eps),
1083	                self.q_norm.weight, self.k_norm.weight,
1084	                float(self.rope_theta), True, pos_i32,
1085	                1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0, self.head_dim,
1086	            )
1087	            _evt_end(e)
1088	            s, e = _evt_record("gla_qkv_split_ms")
1089	            q, k, v = qkv.split([self.q_size, self.kv_size, self.kv_size], dim=-1)
1090	            q = q.contiguous(); k = k.contiguous(); v = v.contiguous()
1091	            _evt_end(e)
1092	            used_fused_qknorm_rope = True
1093	            _gla_oop_done = False
1094	        else:
1095	            _gla_oop_done = False
1096	            s, e = _evt_record("gla_qkv_split_ms"); q, k, v = qkv.split([self.q_size, self.kv_size, self.kv_size], dim=-1); _evt_end(e)
1097	
1098	            if self.qk_norm:

1100	                q = self.q_norm(q.reshape(-1, self.head_dim))
1101	                k = self.k_norm(k.reshape(-1, self.head_dim))
1102	                _evt_end(e)

1994	class SimpleGLAAttnBackend(MambaAttnBackendBase):
1995	    """Attention backend for MiniCPM hybrid models using the SimpleGLA CUDA kernels.
1996	
1997	    This backend assumes the model's ``mixer_types`` includes ``"lightning-attn"`` and
1998	    that the optional ``fla`` package is installed. It does **not** perform any
1999	    convolution or Mamba‑style processing; instead it forwards the query, key and
2000	    value tensors directly to ``parallel_simple_gla``.
2001	
2002	    If the ``fla`` package is missing an ``ImportError`` is raised during
2003	    construction, allowing the caller to fall back to an alternative backend such
2004	    as ``Mamba2AttnBackend``.
2005	    """
2006

2020

2063	            and self._tree_topk is not None and self._tree_topk > 1

2074	    def init_cuda_graph_state(self, max_bs: int, max_num_tokens: int):
2075	        super().init_cuda_graph_state(max_bs, max_num_tokens)
2076	        assert max_num_tokens % max_bs == 0
2077	        draft_token_num = max_num_tokens // max_bs
2078	        self._cg_draft_token_num = draft_token_num
2079	        self._cg_max_bs = max_bs
2080	
2081	        # Pre-allocate tensors needed by TARGET_VERIFY forward path
2082	        self._cg_has_initial_state = torch.ones(
2083	            max_bs, dtype=torch.bool, device=self.device
2084	        )
2085	        self._cg_cu_step = torch.arange(
2086	            0, max_bs + 1, dtype=torch.int32, device=self.device
2087	        )
2088	        # Pre-allocated cu_seqlens for fused kernel (avoids allocation in CUDA graph)
2089	        self._cg_verify_cu_seqlens = torch.arange(
2090	            0, max_bs * draft_token_num + 1, step=draft_token_num,
2091	            dtype=torch.int64, device=self.device,
2092	        )
2093	        # Per-dtn cu_seqlens buffers: dynamic mode captures TARGET_VERIFY graphs
2094	        # at multiple dtn values (D5=5 alongside max=D7=7). The GLA kernel reads
2095	        # cu_seqlens at replay time, so each captured graph must reference a
2096	        # buffer whose contents match its own dtn. Using a single (max-dtn)
2097	        # buffer for D5 leaks D7 stride into D5 graphs and triggers OOB reads.
2098	        import os as _os
2099	        self._cg_verify_cu_seqlens_by_dtn = {draft_token_num: self._cg_verify_cu_seqlens}
2100	        if _os.environ.get("EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE", "0") == "1":
2101	            d5_dtn = int(_os.environ.get("EAGLE_D5_DTN", "5"))
2102	            if d5_dtn != draft_token_num and d5_dtn > 0:
2103	                self._cg_verify_cu_seqlens_by_dtn[d5_dtn] = torch.arange(
2104	                    0, max_bs * d5_dtn + 1, step=d5_dtn,
2105	                    dtype=torch.int64, device=self.device,
2106	                )
2107	        # Pre-allocated buffers for fused GLA kernel (MUST be allocated before
2108	        # CUDA graph capture — tensors allocated inside capture use the graph's
2109	        # private memory pool which can be reused/overwritten between replays)
2110	        K = self.head_dim
2111	        V = self.head_dim
2112	        H = self.num_heads
2113	        total_tokens_max = max_bs * draft_token_num
2114	        BK = min(triton.next_power_of_2(K), 64)
2115	        BV = min(triton.next_power_of_2(V), 64)
2116	        NK = triton.cdiv(K, BK)
2117	        # o_buf: (NK, 1, total_tokens, H, V) — accumulator for k-tiles, summed after kernel
2118	        self._cg_fused_o_buf = torch.empty(
2119	            NK, 1, total_tokens_max, H, V,
2120	            dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device,
2121	        )
2122	        # ht_buf: (max_bs * H, draft_token_num, K, V) — per-step intermediate states
2123	        self._cg_fused_ht_buf = torch.empty(
2124	            max_bs * H, draft_token_num, K, V,
2125	            dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device,
2126	        )
2127	        # o_sum: (1, total_tokens, H, V) — result of o_buf.sum(0), pre-allocated (float32)
2128	        self._cg_fused_o_sum = torch.empty(
2129	            1, total_tokens_max, H, V,
2130	            dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device,
2131	        )
2132	        # Pre-computed constants
2133	        self._cg_fused_NK = NK
2134	        self._cg_fused_BK = BK
2135	        self._cg_fused_BV = BV
2136	
2137	        # --- Per-branch (tree verify) buffers ------------------------------
2138	        if self._per_branch:
2139	            k = self._tree_topk
2140	            # Each req becomes k branches of length 2: [user_4813494d, child_i], total 2*k tokens
2141	            total_tokens_pb = max_bs * 2 * k
2142	            # cu_seqlens for bs*k seqs each length 2
2143	            self._cg_pb_cu_seqlens = torch.arange(
2144	                0, total_tokens_pb + 1, step=2,
2145	                dtype=torch.int64, device=self.device,
2146	            )
2147	            # ht_buf: (bs*k*H, 2, K, V) — 2 steps per branch
2148	            self._cg_pb_ht_buf = torch.empty(
2149	                max_bs * k * H, 2, K, V,
2150	                dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device,
2151	            )
2152	            # o_buf: (NK, 1, bs*2*k, H, V)
2153	            self._cg_pb_o_buf = torch.empty(
2154	                NK, 1, total_tokens_pb, H, V,
2155	                dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device,
2156	            )
2157	            self._cg_pb_o_sum = torch.empty(
2158	                1, total_tokens_pb, H, V,
2159	                dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device,
2160	            )
2161	            # gather_in: pick from flat [user_4813494d, c1, ..., ck] (len dtn=k+1) to expand into
2162	            # [user_4813494d, c1, user_4813494d, c2, ..., user_4813494d, ck] (len 2*k) — per req.
2163	            # per-req pattern: [0, 1, 0, 2, 0, 3, ..., 0, k]

103	if _HAS_TRITON:
104	    @triton.jit
105	    def _gla_sigmoid_mul_kernel(A, B, OUT, n_elements, BLOCK: tl.constexpr):
106	        pid = tl.program_id(0)
107	        offs = pid * BLOCK + tl.arange(0, BLOCK)
108	        mask = offs < n_elements
109	        a = tl.load(A + offs, mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
110	        b = tl.load(B + offs, mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
111	        s = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-b))
112	        y = a * s
113	        tl.store(OUT + offs, y.to(OUT.dtype.element_ty), mask=mask)
114	
115	
116	    def _minicpm_gla_sigmoid_mul(a: torch.Tensor, b: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
117	        """y = a * sigmoid(b) — fused triton kernel for GLA output gate.
118	        Replaces a*F.sigmoid(b) with one kernel (saves 1 intermediate buf).
119	        """
120	        out = torch.empty_like(a)
121	        n = a.numel()
122	        block = 4096
123	        grid = (triton.cdiv(n, block),)
124	        _gla_sigmoid_mul_kernel[grid](a, b, out, n, BLOCK=block,
125	                                       num_warps=2, num_stages=2)
126	        return out
127	
128	
129	    @triton.jit
130	    def _gla_rmsnorm_sigmoid_mul_kernel(O, Z, W, OUT, T_, H_,
131	                                          EPS_, BLOCK_D: tl.constexpr,
132	                                          BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr):
133	        """Each program: BLOCK_M rows. Computes
134	            m[i,j] = o[i,j] * rstd[i] * w[j] * sigmoid(z[i,j])  (fp32 internal)
135	        for o_norm (RMSNorm) + sigmoid_mul fused.
136	        """
137	        block_idx = tl.program_id(0)
138	        rows = block_idx * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
139	        rmask = rows < T_
140	        cols = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_D)
141	        cmask = cols < H_
142	        offs = rows[:, None] * H_ + cols[None, :]
143	        om = (rmask[:, None]) & (cmask[None, :])
144	
145	        o = tl.load(O + offs, mask=om, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
146	        z = tl.load(Z + offs, mask=om, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
147	        w = tl.load(W + cols, mask=cmask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
148	        var = tl.sum(o * o, axis=1) / H_
149	        rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + EPS_)
150	        sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-z))
151	        y = o * rstd[:, None] * w[None, :] * sig
152	        tl.store(OUT + offs, y.to(OUT.dtype.element_ty), mask=om)
153	
154	
155	    def _minicpm_gla_rmsnorm_sigmoid_mul(o, z, w, eps,
156	                                           block_m=4, num_warps=8, num_stages=2):
157	        """Fused o_norm (RMSNorm) + sigmoid_mul.
158	
159	        o, z: (T, H) bf16 contig
160	        w: (H,) bf16 — o_norm.weight
161	        eps: float
162	        Returns: (T, H) bf16 contig
163	        """
164	        T_, H_ = o.shape
165	        out = torch.empty_like(o)
166	        grid = (triton.cdiv(T_, block_m),)
167	        _gla_rmsnorm_sigmoid_mul_kernel[grid](
168	            o, z, w, out, T_, H_, eps,
169	            BLOCK_D=H_, BLOCK_M=block_m,
170	            num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages,
171	        )
172	        return out
173	
174	
175	    @triton.jit
176	    def _gla_qknorm_rope_oop_kernel(
177	        QKV, Q_OUT, K_OUT, V_OUT, Q_W, K_W, COS_SIN, POSITIONS,
178	        T_, NH_Q: tl.constexpr, NH_K: tl.constexpr, NH_V: tl.constexpr,
179	        D_: tl.constexpr, HALF: tl.constexpr, EPS_: tl.constexpr,
180	        BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
181	    ):
182	        """Each program: BLOCK_M tokens of one head, dispatching by head_lin to
183	        Q rmsnorm+rope / K rmsnorm+rope / V passthrough.
184	
185	        Grid: (cdiv(T, BLOCK_M), NH_Q + NH_K + NH_V)
186	        cos_sin layout: (max_pos, D) fp32, [cos[:HALF] | sin[:HALF]] flat per row.
187	        """
188	        block_idx = tl.program_id(0)
189	        head_lin = tl.program_id(1)
190	        total_heads: tl.constexpr = NH_Q + NH_K + NH_V
191	        is_q = head_lin < NH_Q
192	        is_k = (head_lin >= NH_Q) & (head_lin < NH_Q + NH_K)
193	
194	        rows = block_idx * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
195	        rmask = rows < T_
196	        cols = tl.arange(0, D_)
197	        in_offsets = rows[:, None] * (total_heads * D_) + head_lin * D_ + cols[None, :]
198	        x = tl.load(QKV + in_offsets, mask=rmask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
199	
200	        if is_q | is_k:
201	            var = tl.sum(x * x, axis=1) / D_
202	            rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + EPS_)
203	            if is_q:
204	                w = tl.load(Q_W + cols).to(tl.float32)
205	            else:
206	                w = tl.load(K_W + cols).to(tl.float32)
207	            xn = x * rstd[:, None] * w[None, :]
208	
209	            pos = tl.load(POSITIONS + rows, mask=rmask, other=0).to(tl.int32)
210	            is_first = cols < HALF
211	            idx_in_half = tl.where(is_first, cols, cols - HALF)
212	            cos_offs = pos[:, None] * D_ + idx_in_half[None, :]
213	            sin_offs = pos[:, None] * D_ + HALF + idx_in_half[None, :]
214	            cos_full = tl.load(COS_SIN + cos_offs)
215	            sin_full = tl.load(COS_SIN + sin_offs)
216	
217	            pair_idx = tl.where(is_first, cols + HALF, cols - HALF)
218	            pair_offs = rows[:, None] * (total_heads * D_) + head_lin * D_ + pair_idx[None, :]
219	            x_pair_raw = tl.load(QKV + pair_offs, mask=rmask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
220	            if is_q:
221	                w_pair = tl.load(Q_W + pair_idx).to(tl.float32)
222	            else:
223	                w_pair = tl.load(K_W + pair_idx).to(tl.float32)
224	            x_pair = x_pair_raw * rstd[:, None] * w_pair[None, :]
225	            sgn = tl.where(is_first, -1.0, 1.0)
226	            y = xn * cos_full + sgn[None, :] * x_pair * sin_full
227	
228	            if is_q:
229	                out_offsets = rows[:, None] * (NH_Q * D_) + head_lin * D_ + cols[None, :]
230	                tl.store(Q_OUT + out_offsets, y.to(Q_OUT.dtype.element_ty), mask=rmask[:, None])
231	            else:
232	                head_in_k = head_lin - NH_Q
233	                out_offsets = rows[:, None] * (NH_K * D_) + head_in_k * D_ + cols[None, :]
234	                tl.store(K_OUT + out_offsets, y.to(K_OUT.dtype.element_ty), mask=rmask[:, None])
235	        else:
236	            head_in_v = head_lin - NH_Q - NH_K
237	            out_offsets = rows[:, None] * (NH_V * D_) + head_in_v * D_ + cols[None, :]
238	            tl.store(V_OUT + out_offsets, x.to(V_OUT.dtype.element_ty), mask=rmask[:, None])
239	
240	
241	    def _minicpm_fused_qknorm_rope_oop(
242	        qkv: torch.Tensor,
243	        q_w: torch.Tensor,
244	        k_w: torch.Tensor,
245	        cos_sin_cache: torch.Tensor,
246	        positions: torch.Tensor,
247	        nh_q: int, nh_k: int, nh_v: int, head_dim: int, eps: float,
248	        block_m: int = 16, num_warps: int = 2, num_stages: int = 2,
249	    ):
250	        """Returns three contig tensors: q (T,nh_q,D), k (T,nh_k,D), v (T,nh_v,D)."""
251	        T_ = qkv.shape[0]
252	        q_out = torch.empty((T_, nh_q, head_dim), device=qkv.device, dtype=qkv.dtype)
253	        k_out = torch.empty((T_, nh_k, head_dim), device=qkv.device, dtype=qkv.dtype)
254	        v_out = torch.empty((T_, nh_v, head_dim), device=qkv.device, dtype=qkv.dtype)
255	        if positions.dtype != torch.int32:
256	            positions = positions.to(torch.int32)
257	        grid = (triton.cdiv(T_, block_m), nh_q + nh_k + nh_v)
258	        _gla_qknorm_rope_oop_kernel[grid](
259	            qkv, q_out, k_out, v_out, q_w, k_w, cos_sin_cache, positions, T_,
260	            NH_Q=nh_q, NH_K=nh_k, NH_V=nh_v,
261	            D_=head_dim, HALF=head_dim // 2,
262	            EPS_=eps,
263	            BLOCK_M=block_m, num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages,
264	        )
265	        return q_out, k_out, v_out
266	else:
267	    _minicpm_fused_qknorm_rope_oop = None
268	_MM_CUDA_PROFILER = _mm_os.getenv("SGLANG_MINICPM_CUDA_PROFILER", "0") == "1"
269	_MM_CUDA_PROFILER_CHUNKS = int(
270	    _mm_os.getenv("SGLANG_MINICPM_CUDA_PROFILER_CHUNKS", "3")
271	)
272	_MM_CUDA_PROFILER_MIN_TOKENS = int(
273	    _mm_os.getenv("SGLANG_MINICPM_CUDA_PROFILER_MIN_TOKENS", "4096")
274	)
275	_MM_CUDA_PROFILER_STATE = {"started": False, "done": False, "remaining": 0}
276

279	# We record (start_evt, end_evt, bucket) tuples during the forward pass,
280	# then synchronize ONCE at the end to collect GPU-accurate ms durations.

14	def chunk_simple_gla_fwd(
15	    q: torch.Tensor,
16	    k: torch.Tensor,
17	    v: torch.Tensor,
18	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
19	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
20	    scale: float | None = None,
21	    initial_state: torch.Tensor | None = None,
22	    output_final_state: bool = False,
23	    cu_seqlens: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
24	    chunk_size: int = 64,
25	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
26	    h, ht = chunk_fwd_h(
27	        k=k,
28	        v=v,
29	        g=g,
30	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
31	        gk=None,
32	        gv=None,
33	        h0=initial_state,
34	        output_final_state=output_final_state,
35	        states_in_fp32=False,
36	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
37	        chunk_size=chunk_size,
38	    )
39	    o = chunk_fwd_o(
40	        q=q,
41	        k=k,
42	        v=v,
43	        g=g,
44	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
45	        h=h,
46	        scale=scale,
47	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
48	        chunk_size=chunk_size,
49	    )
50	    return o, ht

53	def chunk_simple_gla_bwd(
54	    q: torch.Tensor,
55	    k: torch.Tensor,
56	    v: torch.Tensor,
57	    g: torch.Tensor,
58	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor,
59	    initial_state: torch.Tensor,
60	    do: torch.Tensor,
61	    dht: torch.Tensor,
62	    scale: float,
63	    cu_seqlens: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
64	    chunk_size: int = 64,
65	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
66	    # (SY 09/22) states_in_fp32 seems not affecting the error of dg but for safety, set to True
67	    h, _ = chunk_fwd_h(
68	        k=k,
69	        v=v,
70	        g=g,
71	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
72	        gk=None,
73	        gv=None,
74	        h0=initial_state,
75	        output_final_state=False,
76	        states_in_fp32=True,
77	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
78	        chunk_size=chunk_size,
79	    )
80	    dh, dh0 = chunk_bwd_dh(
81	        q=q,
82	        k=k,
83	        v=v,
84	        g=g,
85	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
86	        gk=None,
87	        gv=None,
88	        do=do,
89	        h0=initial_state,
90	        dht=dht,
91	        scale=scale,
92	        states_in_fp32=True,
93	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
94	        chunk_size=chunk_size,
95	    )
96	    dq, dk, _, dg = chunk_bwd_dqkwg(
97	        q=q,
98	        k=k,
99	        v=v,
100	        g=g,
101	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
102	        h=h,
103	        do=do,
104	        dh=dh,
105	        scale=scale,
106	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
107	        chunk_size=chunk_size,
108	    )
109	    dv = chunk_bwd_dv(
110	        q=q,
111	        k=k,
112	        g=g,
113	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
114	        do=do,
115	        dh=dh,
116	        scale=scale,
117	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
118	        chunk_size=chunk_size,
119	    )
120	    return dq, dk, dv, dg, dh0
121	
122	
123	class ChunkSimpleGLAFunction(torch.autograd.Function):
124	
125	    @staticmethod
126	    @input_guard
127	    @autocast_custom_fwd
128	    def forward(
129	        ctx,
130	        q,
131	        k,
132	        v,
133	        g,
134	        g_gamma,
135	        scale,
136	        initial_state,
137	        output_final_state,
138	        cu_seqlens,
139	    ):
140	        T = q.shape[1]
141	        chunk_size = min(64, max(16, triton.next_power_of_2(T)))
142	
143	        g = chunk_local_cumsum(g, chunk_size=chunk_size, cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens) if g is not None else None
144	        o, ht = chunk_simple_gla_fwd(
145	            q=q,
146	            k=k,
147	            v=v,
148	            g=g,
149	            g_gamma=g_gamma,
150	            scale=scale,
151	            initial_state=initial_state,
152	            output_final_state=output_final_state,
153	            cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
154	            chunk_size=chunk_size,
155	        )
156	        ctx.save_for_backward(q, k, v, g, g_gamma, initial_state)
157	        ctx.chunk_size = chunk_size
158	        ctx.scale = scale
159	        ctx.cu_seqlens = cu_seqlens
160	        return o.to(q.dtype), ht
161	
162	    @staticmethod
163	    @input_guard
164	    @autocast_custom_bwd
165	    def backward(ctx, do, dht):
166	        chunk_size, scale, cu_seqlens = ctx.chunk_size, ctx.scale, ctx.cu_seqlens
167	        q, k, v, g, g_gamma, initial_state = ctx.saved_tensors
168	        dq, dk, dv, dg, dh0 = chunk_simple_gla_bwd(
169	            q=q,
170	            k=k,
171	            v=v,
172	            g=g,
173	            g_gamma=g_gamma,
174	            initial_state=initial_state,
175	            do=do,
176	            dht=dht,
177	            scale=scale,
178	            cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
179	            chunk_size=chunk_size,
180	        )
181	        if g is not None:
182	            dg = chunk_local_cumsum(dg, chunk_size=chunk_size, reverse=True, cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens).to(g)
183	        else:
184	            dg = None
185	        return dq.to(q), dk.to(k), dv.to(v), dg, None, None, dh0, None, None
186	
187	
188	@torch.compiler.disable
189	def chunk_simple_gla(
190	    q: torch.Tensor,
191	    k: torch.Tensor,
192	    v: torch.Tensor,
193	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
194	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
195	    scale: float | None = None,
196	    initial_state: torch.Tensor | None = None,
197	    output_final_state: bool = False,
198	    cu_seqlens: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
199	    head_first: bool = False,
200	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
201	    r"""
202	    Args:
203	        q (torch.Tensor):
204	            queries of shape `[B, T, H, K]`.
205	        k (torch.Tensor):
206	            keys of shape `[B, T, H, K]`.
207	        v (torch.Tensor):
208	            values of shape `[B, T, H, V]`.
209	        g (torch.Tensor):
210	            Forget gates of shape `[B, T, H]`.
211	            Compared to GLA, the gating is head-wise instead of elementwise.
212	        g_gamma (torch.Tensor):
213	            Log decay of shape `[H]`.
214	            Head-wise data-independent decay is used if `g_gamma` is provided.
215	            Only one of `g` or `g_gamma` should be provided.
216	        scale (Optional[float]):
217	            Scale factor for the attention scores.
218	            If not provided, it will default to `1 / sqrt(K)`. Default: `None`.
219	        initial_state (Optional[torch.Tensor]):
220	            Initial state of shape `[N, H, K, V]` for `N` input sequences.
221	            For equal-length input sequences, `N` equals the batch size `B`.
222	            Default: `None`.
223	        output_final_state (Optional[bool]):
224	            Whether to output the final state of shape `[N, H, K, V]`. Default: `False`.
225	        cu_seqlens (torch.LongTensor):
226	            Cumulative sequence lengths of shape `[N+1]` used for variable-length training,
227	            consistent with the FlashAttention API.
228	        head_first (Optional[bool]):
229	            Whether the inputs are in the head-first format. Default: `False`.
230	            This argument has been deprecated.
231	
232	    Returns:
233	        o (torch.Tensor):
234	            Outputs of shape `[B, T, H, V]`.
235	        final_state (torch.Tensor):
236	            Final state of shape `[N, H, K, V]` if `output_final_state=True` else `None`.
237	
238	    Examples::
239	        >>> import torch
240	        >>> import torch.nn.functional as F
241	        >>> from einops import rearrange
242	        >>> from fla.ops.simple_gla import chunk_simple_gla
243	        # inputs with equal lengths
244	        >>> B, T, H, K, V = 4, 2048, 4, 512, 512
245	        >>> q = torch.randn(B, T, H, K, device='cuda')
246	        >>> k = torch.randn(B, T, H, K, device='cuda')
247	        >>> v = torch.randn(B, T, H, V, device='cuda')
248	        >>> g = F.logsigmoid(torch.randn(B, T, H, device='cuda'))
249	        >>> o, ht = chunk_simple_gla(
250	            q, k, v, g,
251	            initial_state=None,
252	            output_final_state=True
253	        )
254	        # for variable-length inputs, the batch size `B` is expected to be 1 and `cu_seqlens` is required
255	        >>> q, k, v, g = map(lambda x: rearrange(x, 'b t ... -> 1 (b t) ...'), (q, k, v, g))
256	        # for a batch with 4 sequences, `cu_seqlens` with 5 start/end positions are expected
257	        >>> cu_seqlens = q.new_tensor([0, 2048, 4096, 6144, 8192], dtype=torch.long)
258	        >>> o_var, ht_var = chunk_simple_gla(
259	            q, k, v, g,
260	            initial_state=None,
261	            output_final_state=True,
262	            cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens
263	        )
264	    """
265	    if head_first:
266	        raise DeprecationWarning(
267	            "head_first is deprecated and will be removed in a future version. "
268	            "Please use head_first=False for now instead.",
269	        )
270	    if not head_first and q.shape[1] < q.shape[2]:
271	        warnings.warn(
272	            f"Input tensor shape suggests potential format mismatch: seq_len ({q.shape[1]}) < num_heads ({q.shape[2]}). "
273	            "This may indicate the inputs were passed in head-first format [B, H, T, ...] "
274	            "when head_first=False was specified. "
275	            "Please verify your input tensor format matches the expected shape [B, T, H, ...].",
276	        )
277	    if cu_seqlens is not None:
278	        if q.shape[0] != 1:
279	            raise ValueError(
280	                f"The batch size is expected to be 1 rather than {q.shape[0]} when using `cu_seqlens`."
281	                f"Please flatten variable-length inputs before processing.",
282	            )
283	        if initial_state is not None and initial_state.shape[0] != len(cu_seqlens) - 1:
284	            raise ValueError(
285	                f"The number of initial states is expected to be equal to the number of input sequences, "
286	                f"i.e., {len(cu_seqlens) - 1} rather than {initial_state.shape[0]}.",
287	            )
288	    if scale is None:
289	        scale = k.shape[-1] ** -0.5
290	    o, final_state = ChunkSimpleGLAFunction.apply(
291	        q,
292	        k,
293	        v,
294	        g,
295	        g_gamma,
296	        scale,
297	        initial_state,
298	        output_final_state,
299	        cu_seqlens,
300	    )
301	    return o, final_state
302

4	import torch
5	import triton
6	import triton.language as tl
7	
8	from fla.ops.utils import prepare_chunk_offsets
9	from fla.ops.utils.op import exp
10	from fla.utils import autotune_cache_kwargs, check_shared_mem
11	
12	BKV_LIST = [32, 64] if check_shared_mem() else [16, 32]
13	
14	
15	@triton.heuristics({
16	    'USE_INITIAL_STATE': lambda args: args['h0'] is not None,
17	    'STORE_FINAL_STATE': lambda args: args['ht'] is not None,
18	    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
19	})
20	@triton.autotune(
21	    configs=[
22	        triton.Config({'BK': BK, 'BV': BV}, num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages)
23	        for BK in BKV_LIST
24	        for BV in BKV_LIST
25	        for num_warps in [1, 2, 4, 8]
26	        for num_stages in [2, 3, 4]
27	    ],
28	    key=['BT', 'USE_G', 'USE_GK', 'USE_GV'],
29	    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
30	)
31	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
32	def chunk_fwd_kernel_h(
33	    k,
34	    v,
35	    h,
36	    g,
37	    g_gamma,
38	    gk,
39	    gv,
40	    h0,
41	    ht,
42	    cu_seqlens,
43	    split_offsets,
44	    T,
45	    H: tl.constexpr,
46	    K: tl.constexpr,
47	    V: tl.constexpr,
48	    BT: tl.constexpr,
49	    BS: tl.constexpr,
50	    BK: tl.constexpr,
51	    BV: tl.constexpr,
52	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
53	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
54	    USE_GK: tl.constexpr,
55	    USE_GV: tl.constexpr,
56	    USE_INITIAL_STATE: tl.constexpr,
57	    STORE_FINAL_STATE: tl.constexpr,
58	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
59	):
60	    i_k, i_v, i_nh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)
61	    i_n, i_h = i_nh // H, i_nh % H
62	    if IS_VARLEN:
63	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
64	        T = eos - bos
65	        NT, NS = tl.cdiv(T, BT), tl.cdiv(T, BS)
66	        boh = tl.load(split_offsets + i_n).to(tl.int32)
67	    else:
68	        bos, eos = i_n * T, i_n * T + T
69	        NT, NS = tl.cdiv(T, BT), tl.cdiv(T, BS)
70	        boh = i_n * NS
71	    NTS = BS // BT
72	
73	    if USE_G_GAMMA:
74	        # decay rate given the head index
75	        b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
76	        b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
77	
78	    # [BK, BV]
79	    b_h = tl.zeros([BK, BV], dtype=tl.float32)
80	    if USE_INITIAL_STATE:
81	        p_h0 = tl.make_block_ptr(h0 + i_nh * K*V, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
82	        b_h = tl.load(p_h0, boundary_check=(0, 1)).to(tl.float32)
83	
84	    for i_t in range(NT):
85	        i_s = i_t // NTS
86	        p_k = tl.make_block_ptr(k + (bos*H + i_h) * K, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
87	        p_v = tl.make_block_ptr(v + (bos*H + i_h) * V, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
88	
89	        o_h = ((boh + i_s) * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
90	        p_h = tl.make_block_ptr(h + o_h, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
91	
92	        if i_t % NTS == 0:
93	            tl.store(p_h, b_h.to(p_h.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
94	        # [BK, BT]
95	        b_k = tl.load(p_k, boundary_check=(0, 1))
96	        # [BT, BV]
97	        b_v = tl.load(p_v, boundary_check=(0, 1))
98	        last_idx = min((i_t + 1) * BT, T) - 1
99	
100	        # scalar decay
101	        if USE_G:
102	            b_g_last = tl.load(g + bos * H + last_idx * H + i_h)
103	            p_g = g + bos*H + (i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)) * H + i_h
104	            b_g = tl.load(p_g, mask=(i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT) < T), other=0.)
105	            b_h *= exp(b_g_last)
106	            b_v = (b_v * exp(b_g_last - b_g)[:, None]).to(b_v.dtype)
107	
108	        if USE_G_GAMMA:
109	            b_g_last = b_gamma * min(BT, T - i_t * BT)
110	            b_h *= exp(b_g_last)
111	            b_v = (b_v * exp(b_g_last - b_g)[:, None]).to(b_v.dtype)
112	
113	        # vector decay, h = Diag(gk) @ h
114	        if USE_GK:
115	            p_gk = tl.make_block_ptr(gk + (bos*H + i_h) * K, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
116	            p_gk_last = gk + (bos + last_idx) * H*K + i_h * K + i_k * BK + tl.arange(0, BK)
117	
118	            b_gk_last = tl.load(p_gk_last, mask=(i_k * BK + tl.arange(0, BK) < K), other=0.)
119	            b_h *= exp(b_gk_last)[:, None]
120	
121	            b_gk = tl.load(p_gk, boundary_check=(0, 1))
122	            b_k = (b_k * exp(b_gk_last[:, None] - b_gk)).to(b_k.dtype)
123	
124	        # vector decay, h = h @ Diag(gv)
125	        if USE_GV:
126	            p_gv = tl.make_block_ptr(gv + (bos*H + i_h) * V, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
127	            p_gv_last = gv + (bos + last_idx) * H*V + i_h * V + i_v * BV + tl.arange(0, BV)
128	
129	            b_gv_last = tl.load(p_gv_last, mask=(i_v * BV + tl.arange(0, BV) < V), other=0.)
130	            b_h *= exp(b_gv_last)[None, :]
131	
132	            b_gv = tl.load(p_gv, boundary_check=(0, 1))
133	            b_v = (b_v * exp(b_gv_last[None, :] - b_gv)).to(b_v.dtype)
134	
135	        b_h += tl.dot(b_k, b_v)
136	
137	    if STORE_FINAL_STATE:
138	        p_ht = tl.make_block_ptr(ht + i_nh * K*V, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
139	        tl.store(p_ht, b_h.to(p_ht.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
140	
141	
142	@triton.heuristics({
143	    'STORE_INITIAL_STATE_GRADIENT': lambda args: args['dh0'] is not None,
144	    'USE_FINAL_STATE_GRADIENT': lambda args: args['dht'] is not None,
145	    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
146	})
147	@triton.autotune(
148	    configs=[
149	        triton.Config({'BK': BK, 'BV': BV}, num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages)
150	        for BK in BKV_LIST
151	        for BV in BKV_LIST
152	        for num_warps in [1, 2, 4, 8]
153	        for num_stages in [2, 3, 4]
154	    ],
155	    key=['BT', 'USE_G', 'USE_GK', 'USE_GV'],
156	    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
157	)
158	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
159	def chunk_bwd_kernel_dh(
160	    q,
161	    g,
162	    g_gamma,
163	    gk,
164	    gv,
165	    do,
166	    dh,
167	    dht,
168	    dh0,
169	    cu_seqlens,
170	    split_offsets,
171	    scale,
172	    T,
173	    HQ: tl.constexpr,
174	    H: tl.constexpr,
175	    K: tl.constexpr,
176	    V: tl.constexpr,
177	    BT: tl.constexpr,
178	    BS: tl.constexpr,
179	    BK: tl.constexpr,
180	    BV: tl.constexpr,
181	    NG: tl.constexpr,
182	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
183	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
184	    USE_GK: tl.constexpr,
185	    USE_GV: tl.constexpr,
186	    STORE_INITIAL_STATE_GRADIENT: tl.constexpr,
187	    USE_FINAL_STATE_GRADIENT: tl.constexpr,
188	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
189	):
190	    i_k, i_v, i_nh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)
191	    i_n, i_hq = i_nh // HQ, i_nh % HQ
192	    i_h = i_hq // NG
193	    if IS_VARLEN:
194	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
195	        T = eos - bos
196	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
197	        NS = tl.cdiv(T, BS)
198	        boh = tl.load(split_offsets + i_n).to(tl.int32)
199	    else:
200	        bos, eos = i_n * T, i_n * T + T
201	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
202	        NS = tl.cdiv(T, BS)
203	        boh = i_n * NS
204	
205	    if USE_G_GAMMA:
206	        b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
207	        b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
208	
209	    # [BK, BV]
210	    b_dh = tl.zeros([BK, BV], dtype=tl.float32)
211	    if USE_FINAL_STATE_GRADIENT:
212	        p_dht = tl.make_block_ptr(dht + i_nh * K*V, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
213	        b_dh += tl.load(p_dht, boundary_check=(0, 1)).to(tl.float32)
214	
215	    for i_t in range(NT - 1, -1, -1):
216	        i_s = i_t // (BS // BT)
217	        o_dh = ((boh + i_s) * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
218	        p_dh = tl.make_block_ptr(dh + o_dh, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
219	
220	        if i_t % (BS // BT) == 0:
221	            tl.store(p_dh, b_dh.to(p_dh.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
222	        last_idx = min(i_t * BT + BT, T) - 1
223	        # [BK, BT]
224	        p_q = tl.make_block_ptr(q + (bos*HQ + i_hq) * K, (K, T), (1, HQ*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
225	        p_do = tl.make_block_ptr(do + (bos*HQ + i_hq) * V, (T, V), (HQ*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
226	        b_q = tl.load(p_q, boundary_check=(0, 1))
227	        b_q = (b_q * scale).to(b_q.dtype)
228	        # [BT, BV]
229	        b_do = tl.load(p_do, boundary_check=(0, 1))
230	
231	        if USE_G:
232	            p_g = g + (bos + i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)) * H + i_h
233	            b_g_last = tl.load(g + (bos + last_idx) * H + i_h)
234	            b_g = tl.load(p_g, mask=(i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT) < T), other=0.)
235	            b_q = (b_q * exp(b_g)[None, :]).to(b_q.dtype)
236	            b_dh *= exp(b_g_last)
237	
238	        if USE_G_GAMMA:
239	            b_g_last = b_gamma * min(BT, T - i_t * BT)
240	            b_q = (b_q * exp(b_g)[None, :]).to(b_q.dtype)
241	            b_dh *= exp(b_g_last)
242	
243	        if USE_GK:
244	            p_gk = tl.make_block_ptr(gk + (bos*H + i_h) * K, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
245	            p_gk_last = gk + (bos + last_idx) * H*K + i_h * K + i_k * BK + tl.arange(0, BK)
246	
247	            b_gk = tl.load(p_gk, boundary_check=(0, 1))
248	            b_q = (b_q * exp(b_gk)).to(b_q.dtype)
249	            b_gk_last = tl.load(p_gk_last, mask=(i_k * BK + tl.arange(0, BK) < K), other=0.)
250	            b_dh *= exp(b_gk_last)[:, None]
251	
252	        if USE_GV:
253	            p_gv = tl.make_block_ptr(gv + (bos*H + i_h) * V, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
254	            p_gv_last = gv + (bos + last_idx) * H*V + i_h * V + i_v * BV + tl.arange(0, BV)
255	
256	            b_gv = tl.load(p_gv, boundary_check=(0, 1))
257	            b_do = (b_do * exp(b_gv))
258	
259	            b_gv_last = tl.load(p_gv_last, mask=(i_v * BV + tl.arange(0, BV) < V), other=0.)
260	            b_dh *= exp(b_gv_last)[None, :]
261	
262	        b_dh += tl.dot(b_q, b_do.to(b_q.dtype))
263	
264	    if STORE_INITIAL_STATE_GRADIENT:
265	        p_dh0 = tl.make_block_ptr(dh0 + i_nh * K*V, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
266	        tl.store(p_dh0, b_dh.to(p_dh0.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
267	
268	
269	def chunk_fwd_h(
270	    k: torch.Tensor,
271	    v: torch.Tensor,
272	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
273	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
274	    gk: torch.Tensor | None = None,
275	    gv: torch.Tensor | None = None,
276	    h0: torch.Tensor | None = None,
277	    output_final_state: bool = False,
278	    cu_seqlens: torch.Tensor | None = None,
279	    chunk_size: int = 64,
280	    split_size: int | None = None,
281	    states_in_fp32: bool = False,
282	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
283	    B, T, H, K, V = *k.shape, v.shape[-1]
284	    BT = chunk_size
285	    BS = BT if split_size is None else split_size
286	    assert BS % BT == 0, f"The `split_size` (got {BS}) must be a multiple of `chunk_size` {BT}"
287	    # N: the actual number of sequences in the batch with either equal or variable lengths
288	    if cu_seqlens is None:
289	        N, NS, split_offsets = B, triton.cdiv(T, BS), None
290	    else:
291	        split_offsets = prepare_chunk_offsets(cu_seqlens, BS)
292	        N, NS = len(cu_seqlens) - 1, split_offsets[-1].item()
293	
294	    h = k.new_empty(B, NS, H, K, V, dtype=k.dtype if not states_in_fp32 else torch.float)
295	    ht = k.new_empty(N, H, K, V, dtype=torch.float) if output_final_state else None
296	    def grid(meta): return (triton.cdiv(K, meta['BK']), triton.cdiv(V, meta['BV']), N * H)
297	    chunk_fwd_kernel_h[grid](
298	        k=k,
299	        v=v,
300	        h=h,
301	        g=g,
302	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
303	        gk=gk,
304	        gv=gv,
305	        h0=h0,
306	        ht=ht,
307	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
308	        split_offsets=split_offsets,
309	        T=T,
310	        H=H,
311	        K=K,
312	        V=V,
313	        BT=BT,
314	        BS=BS,
315	        USE_G=g is not None,
316	        USE_G_GAMMA=g_gamma is not None,
317	        USE_GK=gk is not None,
318	        USE_GV=gv is not None,
319	    )
320	    return h, ht
321	
322	
323	def chunk_bwd_dh(
324	    q: torch.Tensor,
325	    k: torch.Tensor,
326	    v: torch.Tensor,
327	    do: torch.Tensor,
328	    h0: torch.Tensor,
329	    dht: torch.Tensor,
330	    scale: float,
331	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
332	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
333	    gk: torch.Tensor | None = None,
334	    gv: torch.Tensor | None = None,
335	    cu_seqlens: torch.Tensor | None = None,
336	    chunk_size: int = 64,
337	    split_size: int | None = None,
338	    states_in_fp32: bool = False,
339	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
340	    B, T, H, K, V = *k.shape, v.shape[-1]
341	    HQ = q.shape[2]
342	    BT = chunk_size
343	    BS = BT if split_size is None else split_size
344	    assert BS % BT == 0, f"The `split_size` (got {BS}) must be a multiple of `chunk_size` {BT}"
345	    # N: the actual number of sequences in the batch with either equal or variable lengths
346	    # NG: number of groups in GQA
347	    if cu_seqlens is None:
348	        N, NS, split_offsets = B, triton.cdiv(T, BS), None
349	    else:
350	        split_offsets = prepare_chunk_offsets(cu_seqlens, BS)
351	        N, NS = len(cu_seqlens) - 1, split_offsets[-1].item()
352	    NG = HQ // H
353	
354	    dh = k.new_empty(B, NS, HQ, K, V, dtype=k.dtype if not states_in_fp32 else torch.float)
355	    dh0 = torch.empty_like(h0, dtype=torch.float) if h0 is not None else None
356	
357	    def grid(meta): return (triton.cdiv(K, meta['BK']), triton.cdiv(V, meta['BV']), N * H)
358	    chunk_bwd_kernel_dh[grid](
359	        q=q,
360	        g=g,
361	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
362	        gk=gk,
363	        gv=gv,
364	        do=do,
365	        dh=dh,
366	        dht=dht,
367	        dh0=dh0,
368	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
369	        split_offsets=split_offsets,
370	        scale=scale,
371	        T=T,
372	        HQ=HQ,
373	        H=H,
374	        K=K,
375	        V=V,
376	        BT=BT,
377	        BS=BS,
378	        NG=NG,
379	        USE_G=g is not None,
380	        USE_G_GAMMA=g_gamma is not None,
381	        USE_GK=gk is not None,
382	        USE_GV=gv is not None,
383	    )
384	    return dh, dh0
385

29	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
30	def chunk_fwd_kernel_o(
31	    q,
32	    k,
33	    v,
34	    h,
35	    g,
36	    g_gamma,
37	    o,
38	    cu_seqlens,
39	    chunk_indices,
40	    scale,
41	    T,
42	    H: tl.constexpr,
43	    K: tl.constexpr,
44	    V: tl.constexpr,
45	    BT: tl.constexpr,
46	    BK: tl.constexpr,
47	    BV: tl.constexpr,
48	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
49	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
50	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
51	):
52	    i_v, i_t, i_bh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)
53	    i_b, i_h = i_bh // H, i_bh % H
54	
55	    if IS_VARLEN:
56	        i_tg = i_t
57	        i_n, i_t = tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2).to(tl.int32), tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2 + 1).to(tl.int32)
58	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
59	        T = eos - bos
60	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
61	    else:
62	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
63	        i_tg = i_b * NT + i_t
64	        bos, eos = i_b * T, i_b * T + T
65	
66	    # offset calculation
67	    q += (bos * H + i_h) * K
68	    k += (bos * H + i_h) * K
69	    v += (bos * H + i_h) * V
70	    o += (bos * H + i_h) * V
71	    h += (i_tg * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
72	
73	    b_o = tl.zeros([BT, BV], dtype=tl.float32)
74	    b_A = tl.zeros([BT, BT], dtype=tl.float32)
75	
76	    for i_k in range(tl.cdiv(K, BK)):
77	        p_q = tl.make_block_ptr(q, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
78	        p_k = tl.make_block_ptr(k, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
79	        p_h = tl.make_block_ptr(h, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
80	        # [BT, BK]
81	        b_q = tl.load(p_q, boundary_check=(0, 1))
82	        # [BK, BT]
83	        b_k = tl.load(p_k, boundary_check=(0, 1))
84	        # [BK, BV]
85	        b_h = tl.load(p_h, boundary_check=(0, 1))
86	
87	        # [BT, BK] @ [BK, BV] -> [BT, BV]
88	        b_o += tl.dot(b_q, b_h)
89	        # [BT, BK] @ [BK, BT] -> [BT, BT]
90	        b_A += tl.dot(b_q, b_k)
91	
92	    if USE_G:
93	        g += bos * H + i_h
94	        p_g = tl.make_block_ptr(g, (T,), (H,), (i_t * BT,), (BT,), (0,))
95	        b_g = tl.load(p_g, boundary_check=(0,))
96	        b_o = b_o * exp(b_g)[:, None]
97	        b_A = b_A * exp(b_g[:, None] - b_g[None, :])
98	
99	    if USE_G_GAMMA:
100	        b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
101	        b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
102	        b_o = b_o * exp(b_g)[:, None]
103	        b_A = b_A * exp(b_g[:, None] - b_g[None, :])
104	
105	    o_t = i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)
106	    m_t = o_t < T
107	    m_A = (o_t[:, None] >= o_t[None, :]) & (m_t[:, None] & m_t)
108	    b_A = tl.where(m_A, b_A, 0)
109	
110	    p_v = tl.make_block_ptr(v, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
111	    p_o = tl.make_block_ptr(o, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
112	
113	    b_v = tl.load(p_v, boundary_check=(0, 1))
114	    # to fix mma -> mma layout conversion
115	    # already solved by triton v3.2 or higher
116	    b_o = b_o * scale + tl.dot(b_A.to(b_v.dtype), b_v) * scale
117	    tl.store(p_o, b_o.to(p_o.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))

136	def chunk_bwd_kernel_dqkwg(
137	    q,
138	    k,
139	    v,
140	    g,
141	    g_gamma,
142	    h,
143	    do,
144	    dh,
145	    dq,
146	    dk,
147	    dw,
148	    dv,
149	    dg,
150	    cu_seqlens,
151	    chunk_indices,
152	    scale,
153	    B: tl.constexpr,
154	    T,
155	    H: tl.constexpr,
156	    K: tl.constexpr,
157	    V: tl.constexpr,
158	    BT: tl.constexpr,
159	    BK: tl.constexpr,
160	    BV: tl.constexpr,
161	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
162	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
163	    USE_DW: tl.constexpr,
164	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
165	):
166	    i_k, i_t, i_bh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)
167	    i_b, i_h = i_bh // H, i_bh % H
168	
169	    all = B * T
170	    if IS_VARLEN:
171	        i_tg = i_t
172	        i_n, i_t = tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2).to(tl.int32), tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2 + 1).to(tl.int32)
173	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
174	        T = eos - bos
175	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
176	    else:
177	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
178	        i_tg = i_b * NT + i_t
179	        bos, eos = i_b * T, i_b * T + T
180	
181	    # offset calculation
182	    v += (bos * H + i_h) * V
183	    do += (bos * H + i_h) * V
184	    h += (i_tg * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
185	    dh += (i_tg * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
186	    q += (bos * H + i_h) * K
187	    k += (bos * H + i_h) * K
188	    dq += (bos * H + i_h) * K
189	    dk += (bos * H + i_h) * K
190	
191	    # for delta rule only
192	    if USE_DW:
193	        dw += (bos * H + i_h) * K
194	        dv += (bos * H + i_h) * V
195	
196	    if USE_G:
197	        dg += i_k * all * H
198	        b_dg_last = tl.zeros([1], dtype=tl.float32) if USE_G else None
199	    if USE_G_GAMMA:
200	        b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
201	        b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
202	        b_g_last = b_gamma * min(BT, T - i_t * BT)
203	    b_dq = tl.zeros([BT, BK], dtype=tl.float32)
204	    b_dk = tl.zeros([BT, BK], dtype=tl.float32)
205	    b_ds = tl.zeros([BT, BT], dtype=tl.float32)
206	    b_dw = tl.zeros([BT, BK], dtype=tl.float32) if USE_DW else None
207	
208	    for i_v in range(tl.cdiv(V, BV)):
209	        p_v = tl.make_block_ptr(v, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
210	        p_do = tl.make_block_ptr(do, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
211	        p_h = tl.make_block_ptr(h, (V, K), (1, V), (i_v * BV, i_k * BK), (BV, BK), (0, 1))
212	        p_dh = tl.make_block_ptr(dh, (V, K), (1, V), (i_v * BV, i_k * BK), (BV, BK), (0, 1))
213	        # [BT, BV]
214	        b_v = tl.load(p_v, boundary_check=(0, 1))
215	        b_do = tl.load(p_do, boundary_check=(0, 1))
216	        # [BV, BK]
217	        b_h = tl.load(p_h, boundary_check=(0, 1))
218	        b_dh = tl.load(p_dh, boundary_check=(0, 1))
219	        if USE_G:
220	            b_dg_last += (tl.sum(b_h * b_dh))
221	        # [BT, BV] @ [BV, BT] -> [BT, BT]
222	        b_ds += tl.dot(b_do, tl.trans(b_v))
223	        # [BT, BV] @ [BV, BK] -> [BT, BK]
224	        b_dq += tl.dot(b_do, b_h.to(b_do.dtype))
225	        # [BT, BV] @ [BV, BK] -> [BT, BK]
226	        b_dk += tl.dot(b_v, b_dh.to(b_v.dtype))
227	        if USE_DW:
228	            p_dv = tl.make_block_ptr(dv, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
229	            b_dv = tl.load(p_dv, boundary_check=(0, 1))
230	            b_dw += tl.dot(b_dv.to(b_v.dtype), b_h.to(b_v.dtype))
231	
232	    if USE_DW:
233	        p_dw = tl.make_block_ptr(dw, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
234	        tl.store(p_dw, -b_dw.to(p_dw.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
235	
236	    tl.debug_barrier()
237	    p_q = tl.make_block_ptr(q, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
238	    p_k = tl.make_block_ptr(k, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
239	    b_q = tl.load(p_q, boundary_check=(0, 1))
240	    b_k = tl.load(p_k, boundary_check=(0, 1))
241	
242	    p_dq = tl.make_block_ptr(dq, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
243	    p_dk = tl.make_block_ptr(dk, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
244	
245	    o_t = i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)
246	    m_t = o_t < T
247	    m_A = (o_t[:, None] >= o_t[None, :]) & (m_t[:, None] & m_t)
248	    if USE_G:
249	        b_dg = tl.zeros([BT], dtype=tl.float32)
250	        g += bos * H + i_h
251	        dg += bos * H + i_h
252	        p_g = tl.make_block_ptr(g, (T,), (H,), (i_t * BT,), (BT,), (0,))
253	        b_g = tl.load(p_g, boundary_check=(0,))
254	        b_g_last = tl.load(g + (min(i_t * BT + BT, T) - 1) * H)
255	        b_dg_last *= exp(b_g_last)
256	
257	        b_dq = b_dq * exp(b_g)[:, None] * scale
258	        b_dg += tl.sum(b_dq * b_q, axis=1)
259	
260	        b_dk = b_dk * tl.where(m_t, exp(-b_g + b_g_last), 0)[:, None]
261	        b_dg -= tl.sum(b_k * b_dk, axis=1)
262	        b_dg_last += tl.sum(b_dk * b_k)
263	
264	        b_ds = tl.where(m_A, b_ds * exp(b_g[:, None] - b_g[None, :]), 0) * scale
265	        b_ds2 = b_ds * tl.dot(b_q, tl.trans(b_k))
266	        b_dg += tl.sum(b_ds2, axis=1)
267	        b_dg -= tl.sum(b_ds2, axis=0)
268	
269	        b_ds = b_ds.to(b_k.dtype)
270	        # [BT, BK]
271	        b_dq += tl.dot(b_ds, b_k)
272	        b_dk += tl.dot(tl.trans(b_ds), b_q)
273	        p_dg = tl.make_block_ptr(dg, (T,), (H,), (i_t * BT,), (BT,), (0,))
274	        # (SY 09/21) revcumsum in a separate kernel due to strange triton compiler issue
275	        # b_dg = tl.dot(tl.where(o_t[:, None] <= o_t[None, :], 1., 0.), b_dg, allow_tf32=False) + b_dg_last)
276	        b_dg = tl.where(o_t < min(i_t * BT + BT, T) - 1, b_dg, b_dg + b_dg_last)
277	        tl.store(p_dq, b_dq.to(p_dq.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
278	        tl.store(p_dk, b_dk.to(p_dk.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
279	        tl.store(p_dg, b_dg.to(p_dg.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0,))
280	
281	    elif USE_G_GAMMA:
282	        b_dq = b_dq * exp(b_g)[:, None] * scale
283	        b_dk = b_dk * tl.where(m_t, exp(-b_g + b_g_last), 0)[:, None]
284	        b_ds = tl.where(m_A, b_ds * exp(b_g[:, None] - b_g[None, :]), 0) * scale
285	        b_ds = b_ds.to(b_k.dtype)
286	        # [BT, BK]
287	        b_dq += tl.dot(b_ds, b_k)
288	        b_dk += tl.dot(tl.trans(b_ds), b_q)
289	        tl.store(p_dq, b_dq.to(p_dq.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
290	        tl.store(p_dk, b_dk.to(p_dk.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
291	
292	    else:
293	        b_ds = tl.where(m_A, b_ds, 0)
294	        b_ds = b_ds.to(b_k.dtype)
295	        b_dq += tl.dot(b_ds, b_k)
296	        b_dk += tl.dot(tl.trans(b_ds), b_q) * scale
297	        b_dq *= scale
298	        tl.store(p_dq, b_dq.to(p_dq.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
299	        tl.store(p_dk, b_dk.to(p_dk.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
300	
301	
302	@triton.heuristics({
303	    'USE_G': lambda args: args['g'] is not None,
304	    'USE_G_GAMMA': lambda args: args['g_gamma'] is not None,
305	    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
306	})
307	@triton.autotune(
308	    configs=[
309	        triton.Config({}, num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages)
310	        for num_warps in NUM_WARPS
311	        for num_stages in [2, 3, 4]
312	    ],
313	    key=['H', 'K', 'V', 'BT', 'BK', 'BV', 'USE_G', 'USE_G_GAMMA'],
314	    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
315	)
316	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
317	def chunk_bwd_kernel_dv(
318	    q,
319	    k,
320	    g,
321	    g_gamma,
322	    do,
323	    dv,
324	    dh,
325	    cu_seqlens,
326	    chunk_indices,
327	    scale,
328	    T,
329	    H: tl.constexpr,
330	    K: tl.constexpr,
331	    V: tl.constexpr,
332	    BT: tl.constexpr,
333	    BK: tl.constexpr,
334	    BV: tl.constexpr,
335	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
336	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
337	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
338	):
339	    i_v, i_t, i_bh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)
340	    i_b, i_h = i_bh // H, i_bh % H
341	    if IS_VARLEN:
342	        i_tg = i_t
343	        i_n, i_t = tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2).to(tl.int32), tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2 + 1).to(tl.int32)
344	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
345	        T = eos - bos
346	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
347	    else:
348	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
349	        i_tg = i_b * NT + i_t
350	        bos, eos = i_b * T, i_b * T + T
351	
352	    b_dv = tl.zeros([BT, BV], dtype=tl.float32)
353	
354	    # offset calculation
355	    q += (bos * H + i_h) * K
356	    k += (bos * H + i_h) * K
357	    do += (bos * H + i_h) * V
358	    dv += (bos * H + i_h) * V
359	    dh += (i_tg * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
360	
361	    b_A = tl.zeros([BT, BT], dtype=tl.float32)
362	    for i_k in range(tl.cdiv(K, BK)):
363	        p_k = tl.make_block_ptr(k, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
364	        p_q = tl.make_block_ptr(q, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
365	        b_q = tl.load(p_q, boundary_check=(0, 1))
366	        b_k = tl.load(p_k, boundary_check=(0, 1))
367	        b_A += tl.dot(b_k, b_q)
368	        p_dh = tl.make_block_ptr(dh, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
369	        b_dh = tl.load(p_dh, boundary_check=(0, 1))
370	        b_dv += tl.dot(b_k, b_dh.to(b_k.dtype))
371	
372	    o_t = i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)
373	    m_t = o_t < T
374	    if USE_G:
375	        g += bos * H + i_h
376	        p_g = tl.make_block_ptr(g, (T,), (H,), (i_t * BT,), (BT,), (0,))
377	        b_g = tl.load(p_g, boundary_check=(0,))
378	        b_g_last = tl.load(g + (min(i_t * BT + BT, T) - 1) * H)
379	    if USE_G_GAMMA:
380	        b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
381	        b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
382	        b_g_last = b_gamma * min(BT, T - i_t * BT)
383	
384	    m_A = (o_t[:, None] <= o_t[None, :]) & (m_t[:, None] & m_t)
385	    if USE_G or USE_G_GAMMA:
386	        b_A = tl.where(m_A, b_A * exp(b_g[None, :] - b_g[:, None]) * scale, 0).to(do.dtype.element_ty)
387	        b_dv *= tl.where(m_t, exp(-b_g + b_g_last), 0)[:, None]
388	    else:
389	        b_A = tl.where(m_A, b_A * scale, 0).to(do.dtype.element_ty)
390	    p_do = tl.make_block_ptr(do, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
391	    p_dv = tl.make_block_ptr(dv, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
392	    b_do = tl.load(p_do, boundary_check=(0, 1))
393	    b_dv += tl.dot(b_A.to(b_do.dtype), b_do)
394	    tl.store(p_dv, b_dv.to(p_dv.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
395	
396	
397	@triton.heuristics({
398	    'USE_G': lambda args: args['g'] is not None,
399	    'USE_G_GAMMA': lambda args: args['g_gamma'] is not None,
400	    'USE_A': lambda args: args['A'] is not None,
401	    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
402	})
403	@triton.autotune(
404	    configs=[
405	        triton.Config({}, num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages)
406	        for num_warps in NUM_WARPS
407	        for num_stages in [2, 3, 4]
408	    ],
409	    key=['H', 'K', 'V', 'BT', 'BK', 'BV', 'USE_G'],
410	    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
411	)
412	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
413	def chunk_bwd_kernel_dv_local(
414	    q,
415	    k,
416	    g,
417	    g_gamma,
418	    A,
419	    do,
420	    dv,
421	    cu_seqlens,
422	    chunk_indices,
423	    scale,
424	    T,
425	    H: tl.constexpr,
426	    K: tl.constexpr,
427	    V: tl.constexpr,
428	    BT: tl.constexpr,
429	    BK: tl.constexpr,
430	    BV: tl.constexpr,
431	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
432	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
433	    USE_A: tl.constexpr,
434	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
435	):
436	    i_t, i_bh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1)
437	    i_b, i_h = i_bh // H, i_bh % H
438	    if IS_VARLEN:
439	        i_n, i_t = tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2).to(tl.int32), tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2 + 1).to(tl.int32)
440	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
441	        T = eos - bos
442	    else:
443	        bos, eos = i_b * T, i_b * T + T
444	
445	    # offset calculation
446	    q += (bos * H + i_h) * K
447	    k += (bos * H + i_h) * K
448	    do += (bos * H + i_h) * V
449	    dv += (bos * H + i_h) * V
450	
451	    if USE_A:
452	        p_A = tl.make_block_ptr(A + (bos * H + i_h) * BT, (BT, T), (1, H*BT), (0, i_t * BT), (BT, BT), (0, 1))
453	        b_A = tl.load(p_A, boundary_check=(0, 1))
454	    else:
455	        if USE_G:
456	            g += bos * H + i_h
457	            p_g = tl.make_block_ptr(g, (T,), (H,), (i_t * BT,), (BT,), (0,))
458	            b_g = tl.load(p_g, boundary_check=(0,))
459	        if USE_G_GAMMA:
460	            b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
461	            b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
462	
463	        b_A = tl.zeros([BT, BT], dtype=tl.float32)
464	        for i_k in range(tl.cdiv(K, BK)):
465	            p_k = tl.make_block_ptr(k, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
466	            p_q = tl.make_block_ptr(q, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
467	
468	            b_k = tl.load(p_k, boundary_check=(0, 1))
469	            b_q = tl.load(p_q, boundary_check=(0, 1))
470	            b_A += tl.dot(b_k, b_q) * scale
471	        if USE_G or USE_G_GAMMA:
472	            b_A *= exp(b_g[None, :] - b_g[:, None])
473	
474	    o_t = i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)
475	    m_t = o_t < T
476	    m_A = (o_t[:, None] <= o_t[None, :]) & (m_t[:, None] & m_t)
477	    b_A = tl.where(m_A, b_A, 0).to(do.dtype.element_ty)
478	
479	    for i_v in range(tl.cdiv(V, BV)):
480	        p_do = tl.make_block_ptr(do, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
481	        p_dv = tl.make_block_ptr(dv, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
482	        b_do = tl.load(p_do, boundary_check=(0, 1))
483	        b_dv = tl.dot(b_A.to(b_do.dtype), b_do)
484	        tl.store(p_dv, b_dv.to(p_dv.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
485	
486	
487	def chunk_fwd_o(
488	    q: torch.Tensor,
489	    k: torch.Tensor,
490	    v: torch.Tensor,
491	    h: torch.Tensor,
492	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
493	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
494	    scale: float | None = None,
495	    cu_seqlens: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
496	    chunk_size: int = 64,
497	) -> torch.Tensor:
498	    B, T, H, K, V = *q.shape, v.shape[-1]
499	    BT = chunk_size
500	    chunk_indices = prepare_chunk_indices(cu_seqlens, BT) if cu_seqlens is not None else None
501	    NT = triton.cdiv(T, BT) if cu_seqlens is None else len(chunk_indices)
502	    if scale is None:
503	        scale = k.shape[-1] ** -0.5
504	
505	    o = torch.empty_like(v)
506	    def grid(meta): return (triton.cdiv(V, meta['BV']), NT, B * H)
507	    chunk_fwd_kernel_o[grid](
508	        q=q,
509	        k=k,
510	        v=v,
511	        h=h,
512	        g=g,
513	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
514	        o=o,
515	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
516	        chunk_indices=chunk_indices,
517	        scale=scale,
518	        T=T,
519	        H=H,
520	        K=K,
521	        V=V,
522	        BT=BT,
523	    )
524	    return o
525	
526	
527	def chunk_bwd_dv(
528	    q: torch.Tensor,
529	    k: torch.Tensor,
530	    do: torch.Tensor,
531	    dh: torch.Tensor,
532	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
533	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
534	    scale: float | None = None,
535	    cu_seqlens: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
536	    chunk_size: int = 64,
537	) -> torch.Tensor:
538	    B, T, H, K, V = *k.shape, do.shape[-1]
539	    BT = chunk_size
540	    chunk_indices = prepare_chunk_indices(cu_seqlens, BT) if cu_seqlens is not None else None
541	    # H100 can have larger block size
542	    if check_shared_mem('hopper', k.device.index):
543	        CONST_TILING = 128
544	    elif check_shared_mem:
545	        CONST_TILING = 64
546	    else:
547	        CONST_TILING = 32
548	    BK = min(max(triton.next_power_of_2(K), 16), CONST_TILING)
549	    BV = min(max(triton.next_power_of_2(V), 16), CONST_TILING)
550	    NT = triton.cdiv(T, BT) if cu_seqlens is None else len(chunk_indices)
551	    NV = triton.cdiv(V, BV)
552	    if scale is None:
553	        scale = k.shape[-1] ** -0.5
554	
555	    dv = torch.empty_like(do)
556	    grid = (NV, NT, B * H)
557	    chunk_bwd_kernel_dv[grid](
558	        q=q,
559	        k=k,
560	        g=g,
561	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
562	        do=do,
563	        dv=dv,
564	        dh=dh,
565	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
566	        chunk_indices=chunk_indices,
567	        scale=scale,
568	        T=T,
569	        H=H,
570	        K=K,
571	        V=V,
572	        BT=BT,
573	        BK=BK,
574	        BV=BV,
575	    )
576	    return dv
577	
578	
579	def chunk_bwd_dv_local(
580	    q: torch.Tensor,
581	    k: torch.Tensor,
582	    do: torch.Tensor,
583	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
584	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
585	    A: torch.Tensor | None = None,
586	    scale: float = None,
587	    cu_seqlens: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
588	    chunk_size: int = 64,
589	    chunk_indices: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
590	) -> torch.Tensor:
591	    B, T, H, K, V = *k.shape, do.shape[-1]
592	    BT = chunk_size
593	    if chunk_indices is None and cu_seqlens is not None:
594	        chunk_indices = prepare_chunk_indices(cu_seqlens, BT)
595	    # H100 can have larger block size
596	    if check_shared_mem('hopper', k.device.index):
597	        CONST_TILING = 128
598	    elif check_shared_mem:
599	        CONST_TILING = 64
600	    else:
601	        CONST_TILING = 32
602	    BK = min(max(triton.next_power_of_2(K), 16), CONST_TILING)
603	    BV = min(max(triton.next_power_of_2(V), 16), CONST_TILING)
604	    NT = triton.cdiv(T, BT) if cu_seqlens is None else len(chunk_indices)
605	
606	    dv = torch.empty_like(do)
607	    grid = (NT, B * H)
608	    chunk_bwd_kernel_dv_local[grid](
609	        q=q,
610	        k=k,
611	        g=g,
612	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
613	        A=A,
614	        do=do,
615	        dv=dv,
616	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
617	        chunk_indices=chunk_indices,
618	        scale=scale,
619	        T=T,
620	        H=H,
621	        K=K,
622	        V=V,
623	        BT=BT,
624	        BK=BK,
625	        BV=BV,
626	    )
627	    return dv
628	
629	
630	def chunk_bwd_dqkwg(
631	    q: torch.Tensor,
632	    k: torch.Tensor,
633	    v: torch.Tensor,
634	    do: torch.Tensor,
635	    h: torch.Tensor,
636	    dh: torch.Tensor,
637	    w: torch.Tensor | None = None,
638	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
639	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
640	    dv: torch.Tensor | None = None,
641	    scale: float | None = None,
642	    cu_seqlens: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
643	    chunk_size: int = 64,
644	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
645	
646	    B, T, H, K, V = *k.shape, v.shape[-1]
647	    BT = chunk_size
648	    chunk_indices = prepare_chunk_indices(cu_seqlens, BT) if cu_seqlens is not None else None
649	    NT = triton.cdiv(T, BT) if cu_seqlens is None else len(chunk_indices)
650	
651	    CONST_TILING = 64 if check_shared_mem() else 32
652	    BK = min(max(triton.next_power_of_2(K), 16), CONST_TILING)
653	    BV = min(max(triton.next_power_of_2(V), 16), CONST_TILING)
654	    NK = triton.cdiv(K, BK)
655	    dq = torch.empty_like(q)
656	    dk = torch.empty_like(k)
657	    dg = torch.empty(NK, *g.shape, dtype=torch.float32, device=g.device) if g is not None else None
658	    dw = torch.empty_like(w) if w is not None else None
659	
660	    grid = (NK, NT, B * H)
661	    chunk_bwd_kernel_dqkwg[grid](
662	        q=q,
663	        k=k,
664	        v=v,
665	        g=g,
666	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
667	        h=h,
668	        do=do,
669	        dh=dh,
670	        dw=dw,
671	        dq=dq,
672	        dk=dk,
673	        dv=dv,
674	        dg=dg,
675	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
676	        chunk_indices=chunk_indices,
677	        scale=scale,
678	        B=B,
679	        T=T,
680	        H=H,
681	        K=K,
682	        V=V,
683	        BT=BT,
684	        BK=BK,
685	        BV=BV,
686	    )
687	
688	    if dg is not None:
689	        dg = dg.sum(0)
690	    return dq, dk, dw, dg
691

1	# Prefill 优化当前事实
2	
3	目标场景：长上下文 prefill，`--chunked-prefill-size 8192`，`--dense-as-sparse`，`minicpm_flashinfer`。线上快速样本：`bench/data/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl` line 39，约 15 chunks，`max_tokens=1`。完整 bench：`python3 bench/kernels/prefill/prefill_bench_smax64.py`。
4	
5	## 1. 当前热点
6	
7	profile 口径：`SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE=1 SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE_INTERVAL=16`。15chunk full-chunk profile（line 39）：
8	
9	| 模块 | ms/chunk |
10	|---|---|
11	| MLP（gate_up + fused act_quant_down） | 133 |
12	| GLA（qkv + chunk_kernel） | 96 |
13	| sparse-FA（extend_sparse_fa） | 84 |
14	| stage1（score） | 74（末段） |
15	
16	15chunk wall 稳态 ≈ **6.78 s**（`max_tokens=1`）。
17	
18	**硬边界**：不能再把 topk / candidate 缩限作为优化方向。任何减少候选集合、截断 topk、近似 fused topk 默认不满足一致性，除非先证明逐 token/topk 完全一致。
19	
20	## 2. 已落地默认优化
21	
22	每条均默认开启；env 是回退/调试旋钮。
23	
24	### 2.1 FlashInfer plan cache
25	`minicpm_attention_kernels.py`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE=1` / `..._CROSS_FORWARD_BUFFER_REFRESH=1`。跨 forward 命中时刷新 `wrapper._paged_kv_*_buf`，否则 stale page table 会 cross-request KV 污染。
26	
27	### 2.2 全 sparse metadata 快路径
28	`minicpm_sparse_utils.py`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_ALL_SPARSE_PREFILL_METADATA=1`。`len(sparse_bs_list)==bs` 时 `cu_seqlens_q=arange`，page-table 向量化覆盖；混合 batch 走原逻辑。
29	
30	### 2.3 sparse seqlens 从 topk 派生
31	`minicpm_backend.py`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_DERIVED_SPARSE_SEQLENS=1`。不能固定 `num_sparse_topk_tokens`（early token 会被 causal 截断）。按 `clamp(min(seqlen_k_sparse_bs, token_pos_in_bs) - topk_idx * block_size, 0, block_size)` 求和。
32	
33	### 2.4 direct sparse page table
34	`minicpm_backend.py`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_DIRECT_SPARSE_PAGE_TABLE=1`。全 sparse 下跳过 `metadata.sparse_page_table[...]=...` 复制。
35	
36	### 2.5 stage1 actual maxlen + direct pool + skip extra zero
37	`minicpm_sparse_utils.py`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_STAGE1_*=1`。**可以**缩 `infllmv2_attn_stage1.max_seqlen_k`；**不能**缩 `max_pooling_1d_varlen.max_context_len`（pooler layout 语义）。首 chunk 候选不足必须 fallback full-layout scratch。ctx=131072 单层 10.39→10.23 ms。
38	
39	### 2.6 prefill block table v3
40	`SGLANG_MINICPM_PREFILL_BLOCK_TABLE_V3=1`。guard `min(seq_lens_cpu) >= num_sparse_topk_tokens`（`get_block_table_v3` 无 `topk_idx=-1` guard，带 `-1` 必 CUDA illegal）。`sparse_block_table_prefill_ms` 12.5→8.8。
41	
42	### 2.7 pool/topk 小项清理
43	`SGLANG_MINICPM_POOL_EMPTY_OUTPUT=1` / `_TOPK_UNSORTED_SELECT=1`。严格等价；wall +0.1%。
44	
45	### 2.8 GLA qkv cuDNN shape gate
46	`modelopt_quant.py`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_FP4_GLA_QKV_CUDNN=1` 仅 `x_m==8192` 命中；启动预热 `M=8192,K=4096,N=12288`（避免首 chunk 200ms cudnn 初始化）。全局 `SGLANG_FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND=cudnn` 已否决（MLP gate_up 变慢）。收益：wall +2.4%。

49	`sgl_kernel.silu_and_mul_scaled_fp4_grouped_quant`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_FUSED_MLP_ACT_QUANT=1`。guard：CUDA、2D contig gate_up、无 bias / pre_quant_scale、不走 Marlin。收益：wall +1.5%。

52	`minicpm_sparse_stage2.py::triton_topk_to_flashinfer_indices`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_TOPK_TO_FI_INDICES=1`。guard 全 sparse + full-topk + `block_size=64`。`sparse_block_table_prefill_ms=0`，`fi_convert_ms` 5.2→0.6。收益：wall +3-4%。
53	
54	### 2.11 InfLLM-v2 stage1 full-chunk trait guard
55	`kernels/infllmv2_cuda_impl/csrc/flash_attn/src/flash_fwd_launch_template.h`。仅当 `seqlen_q % 32 == 0 && total_q == seqlen_q * b` 走 `32x64,2w`；tail / mixed-q fallback `16x64,1w`。早期全局换 `32x64` 在 `T=2747` tail 出 `diff_max=0.0046`、topk 不一致。落地 `demo-sala/prebuilt/infllm_v2_C.cpython-310-x86_64-linux-gnu.so`。**stage1 trait sweep 已枯竭**，要继续必须算法/语义级改动。

58	`SGLANG_MINICPM_FUSED_GLA_QK_NORM_ROPE=0`。替代 baseline 3 kernel（rmsnorm q/k + apply_rope_with_cos_sin_cache）。in-place 后 `qkv.split + 3x .contiguous()` 引 8.74 ms gla_qkv_split。**§2.13 OOP 严格优于**，本条仅作 fallback。
59

61	`minicpm.py::_minicpm_fused_qknorm_rope_oop`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_FUSED_GLA_QK_NORM_ROPE_OOP=1`。triton 一 launch 写 q_out/k_out/v_out 三 contig 输出，消除 §2.12 的 split + contig 开销。`BLOCK_M=16 num_warps=2`。`attn_gla_ms` 124.7→96.4（-28 ms/chunk）。
62	
63	### 2.14 GLA fused o_norm + sigmoid + mul（3-in-1 triton）
64	`minicpm.py::_minicpm_gla_rmsnorm_sigmoid_mul`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_GLA_FUSED_RMSNORM_SIGMOID_MUL=1`。`m = o*rstd*w*sigmoid(z)` 全 fp32。`BLOCK_M=4 num_warps=4`。`o_norm 1.96 + sigmoid_mul 2.44 = 4.4` → 3.47 ms/chunk。
65	
66	### 2.15 GLA 优化累计
67	baseline med 6.830 → 当前 med 6.775 s（**-0.81%**）。GLA 累计 -27 ms cuda time / chunk × 15 chunks = -405 ms，wall 受 GPU clock / launch queue 噪声稀释。

69	`chunk_simple_gla 27.6ms` 是 GLA 不可压缩下限（fla `chunk_h + chunk_o` 已 BW-bound）。

71	### 2.16 GLA z_proj 与 gla_kernel 多 stream 并发（R-prefill-01）
72	`minicpm.py::MiniCPMLightningMixer.forward`。`SGLANG_MINICPM_GLA_ZPROJ_STREAM=1`。z_proj 走 side stream，与 gla_kernel 并发；sync 在 rmsnorm_sigmoid_mul 前。z 只读 hidden_states，gla_kernel 只读 q/k/v，无数据依赖。`main.wait_stream(side)` + `z.record_stream(main)` 防 caching allocator 误回收。单例 stream 全模型共享。

87	### 3.1 fi_convert 跨层缓存

90	### 3.2 TrtLLM stage2 替换

151	
152	### 3.21 Triton 直写 stage2
153	生产 shape `triton_direct=10.37` vs FlashInfer 10.13 ms，慢 ~2.4%。tile sweep 仍慢 18%。要继续需更接近 FlashInfer 的 split/reduce 结构。
154	
155	### 3.22 stage2 kv dtype 校准
156	线上是 bf16 KV，生产形态 `default+TC=10.04` ms 与线上 9.8-10.0 对齐。旧 fp8 口径 ~7.6 ms 不再当 baseline。
157	
158	### 3.23 MLP dense-as-MoE fused path
159	用 FlashInfer `cutlass_fused_moe` 把 dense 当 1-expert MoE。`M=8192 +1.0%` 单 GEMM 折 32 层约 1.9 ms/chunk，低于接入复杂度（FlashInfer cached_ops 192MB / `MAX_JOBS=1` 编译 80s）。不进生产。真有价值的 MLP 方向是 gated FP4 epilogue（§3.24）。
160	
161	### 3.24 MLP gate_up epilogue 融合调查
162	核心：让 gate_up GEMM epilogue 直接输出 down_proj 所需 FP4 activation + scale，消掉 BF16 中间态（512MB/chunk）。
163	
164	底层边界：
165	- FlashInfer SM120 FP4 GEMM 现行 epilogue 是 `LinearCombination`，只输出 BF16/FP16。
166	- CUTLASS SM120 已有 `LinCombBlockScaleFactor` / `Sm120BlockScaleFactorRowStore`，但 epilogue 是逐元素 unary activation，不能表达 SwiGLU 的 pairwise `up*silu(gate)`。
167	- cuDNN `gemm_swiglu_wrapper_sm100` / FlashInfer CuteDSL SM100/103 语义对，但在 sm120 报 `Arch.sm_120a` 拒绝。
168	- TensorRT-LLM SM120 grouped MoE 明确 `TMA WS doesn't support fused activation`（dense-as-MoE 仅 1% 收益的原因）。
169	
170	SM120 blockscale epilogue 探针（`M=1024,K=4096,N=4096`）：baseline FP4→BF16 0.0935 + BF16 post quant 0.0203 = 0.114 ms vs direct FP4 blockscale 0.0620 ms。`max_diff=0.0072 mean_diff=0.000895`（非 bitwise，FP4 量化舍入差异可接受）。
171	
172	已否决的"先 FP4 gate/up，再 dequant→SwiGLU→requant"路径：合计 0.151→0.227 ms（更慢），`mean_diff=1.85 > ref_abs_mean=1.37`。**不能先把 gate/up 中间态压成 FP4 再做 SwiGLU**。
173	
174	下一步：做 SM120 dense-only `gate_up_swiglu_fp4` kernel（复用 FlashInfer FP4 GEMM mainloop，新增 gated blockscale epilogue：epilogue 取成对 accumulator 算 `silu(gate)*up`，输出直接兼容 `down_proj.forward_fp4_quantized`）。归 docs/gemm 主线。
175	
176	### 3.25 GLA z_proj early-start（R-prefill-06 REJECT）
177	把 §2.16 的 z_proj dispatch 从 qkv_proj 之后挪到顶部，扩 overlap 窗口到 `qkv_proj + fused_qk_norm_rope + gla_kernel`。med 6.3577→6.3850（**+0.43% 反向**）。
178	
179	根因：z_proj 与 qkv_proj 都是 `(M=8192, K=4096)` NVFP4 GEMM，并发跑相同算子两 stream 抢 SM；§2.16 的 z_proj 在 qkv_proj 完成后才上 side stream，与 gla_kernel（chunk_simple_gla，不同算子）并发，SM 调度不冲突。
180	
181	教训（**stream overlap 设计原则**）：两条 stream 跑**相同/相近** GEMM 会 SM 抢占；用 stream 让 side kernel 与 main 的**不同算子**并发；早 dispatch ≠ 早完成。同 GEMM 双 stream 模式封死。
182	
183	### 3.26 Inter-chunk SGLang scheduler pipelining
184	R-07 nsys 3 chunks chunk 边界最大 GPU idle gap 29.9 ms / 8.1 ms。生产 14 chunks ≈ 178 ms ≈ 2.8% wall 理论 ceiling。触达方案：① scheduler 让 next-chunk batch prep 与 current-chunk tail kernels 并行；② 全 8192 token full chunk CUDAGraph capture/replay 去除 ~14 ms / chunk launch overhead。归 sglang fork 主线。**已在 §6 起 sprint**。
